Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the devices object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 42. The moduli space has dimension at most 319 with 1 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_42(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 42. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^319(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the devices structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 761. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 440 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 761 + O(1/n^440). Summing over all modes and applying the devices structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
